Freight Chain · Verification & Quality Engineering Reference

Full Test Coverage, Mocks, Skills & Gaps Audit

Complete empirical review of every unit test, PostgreSQL integration & concurrency spec, HTTP scenario, 60 Hz deterministic headless playthrough, 3D scene-tree parity gate, Solana Anchor LiteSVM & localnet suite, multi-agent security audit harness, browser CDP E2E script, test double, and .claude/skills/ workflow in the monorepo.

01A · Test Report Card (Graded F to A+ by Test Type)

Overall Project Test Grade: A− (91 / 100)

Each test layer is graded from F to A+ based on five criteria: (1) live pass/fail status today, (2) statement & branch coverage of its target layer, (3) realism vs. mock blind spots, (4) concurrency & adversarial edge-case rigor, and (5) enforcement in default check gates.

Test Type / Layer Grade Score Files / Suites Cases / Checks Layer Coverage Strengths & Why It Earned This Grade (Deductions in Bold)
1. PostgreSQL & Redis Integration + Concurrency A+ 98 / 100 49 549 cases (54 races) 100% of PG repos & locks Gold standard of the repo. Zero persistence mocks; tests all 42 Prisma repos, 227 HTTP flow cases on real SQL, 20-way SIWS replay/race attacks, Redis 7 Lua ACLs, and 54 8-way concurrency races verifying SELECT ... FOR UPDATE lock order (player → pool → tickets/pledges) and advisory locks.
2. Architecture, Contract & Migration Audits A 96 / 100 7 40 static audits 100% of server/src & client/js Enforces Clean Architecture inward imports, <= 500 lines/file, no Date.now()/Math.random()/process.env leaks, mandatory @ZodBody pipes, stats.recordRide hooks, 1:1 spec pyramid, forward-only SQL migrations, and acyclic client modules. Deduction: test-pyramid.spec.ts skips src/cli/.
3. Blockchain / Solana On-Chain Tests (LiteSVM + Unit) A 95 / 100 16 197 #[test] (184 LiteSVM) All 30 pools & 14 honk ixs Executes real SBPF v0 binaries in LiteSVM 0.10: 625-combination (5^4) adversarial Merkle prize matrix, 50-run pledge conservation fuzzing, forged u64::MAX overflow guards, v3→v4 account truncation upgrades, and shared JSON fixtures locking Rust ⇄ TS byte parity. Deduction: program-check only pins IDL/hash for pools, not honk.
4. Server Domain, Use-Cases & Services Unit Tests A 95 / 100 245 1,195 cases 97.9% lines · 86.2% branches Fast (~12s), dense coverage of pure domain rules, 10 Hz ReplayEngine traffic/physics parity, anti-cheat verdicts, and CQRS handlers via unitContext(). Deduction: nft-metadata-uris.ts untested; MemoryTransaction.run does not roll back partial writes on throw in unit tests.
5. Client 60 Hz Deterministic Headless Playthrough A 94 / 100 1 87 static / 97 runtime check() 87.9% sim · 97.3% net · 94.8% cockpit Bundles js/main.js with stub-three.mjs and drives 60+ km at 60 Hz in 1.8s across 16 gameplay phases (depot turn, signals, clutch/gear stalls at −22 °C, brake heat, battery/alternator, 30+ cosmetics, 4-step tutorial, stuck-bus recovery, fog/frost). Deduction: Overrides CFG.TRUCK for the first 60 km lane-keeper.
6. Multi-Agent Security Audit Suite (audit/) A− 91 / 100 46 245 cases (208 JS + 37 Rust) 46 / 55 audit scripts Rigorous unit + real-Docker verification of network-isolated sandboxes (--network none --read-only --cap-drop ALL), MCP tool locks, secret/symlink stripping, and native Rust CLI admission gates. Deduction: Omitted from root npm run check and test-census; 9 helper scripts lack unit tests.
7. Landing, Whitepaper, Entity MCP & Media Tests B+ 88 / 100 20 192 cases 99.3% MCP · 90.2% Media · 80.9% Landing Enforces gzip bundle budgets, whitepaper-to-server rule parity, 16-bit WAV/MP3 byte parity, and MCP redirect/SSRF/rate-limit safety. Deduction: 4 pitchdeck visual modules (core, fx, road, ticker), mcp/src/stdio.js, and 3 browser video-capture scripts lack unit tests.
8. Server Infrastructure, Controllers & CLI Unit Tests B+ 87 / 100 126 581 cases 95.2% Infra · 99.8% Ctrl · 51.3% CLI Strong unit tests for Solana codecs, Helius webhooks, SNS adversarial parsing, Redis Lua scripts, and HTTP guards. Deduction: cli/admin-commands.ts (167 lines), cli/cli.ts (106 lines), and app/editor-worker.ts have 0% unit coverage; 7 DTO files are skipped in controller unit specs because busMock() bypasses Nest pipes.
9. Server HTTP Scenarios & In-Memory HTTP Specs B 83 / 100 33 253 cases (×2 backends) 134 / 151 HTTP routes (88.7%) Runs 30 scenario suites identically against both MemoryDb and PostgreSQL, plus dedicated CORS/CSRF, split-host, and rate-limit specs. Deduction: 17 of 151 controller routes (11.3%) have ZERO functional HTTP scenario coverage; analyticsScenarios is registered twice in both runners.
10. Client & Site Unit Tests (client/test/) B 82 / 100 31 166 cases 99.1% shared · 63.8% site · 33.9% UI client/shared/ (entities, road, traffic, physics) is at 99.1% line coverage; wallet/SIWS/SNS/radio/input-bits are well tested. Deduction: 114 of 171 client/js/ files (watch/live overlays, handheld map, editor UI) and client/site/wallet-picker.js have no dedicated unit test file; themes/_shared/profile.js is dead code.
11. Browser Chrome CDP & Localnet E2E Harnesses B− 79 / 100 27 868 static / 940+ runtime check() Full stack (Chrome + PG + Validator) Extraordinary real-stack breadth across 27 scripts (WebGL, WebAudio, anti-cheat bots, 4 localnet escrow suites with RPC fault proxy, TEN4 chain/session suites). Deduction: test:tickets-stub currently FAILS (18/20) because stub-api.mjs returns 404 on /reconcile and /api/referrals; 3–4 concurrent SwiftShader Chromes cause CPU slowdowns against tight 8s–35s pool deadlines (6 failures in last recorded runs.json E2E run).
12. 3D Scene-Tree Parity Gate & test-census Tooling D+ 67 / 100 2 tools 11,643 3D objs + 35 self-tests Meta-verification & refactor gates Clever zero-dependency architecture, but both fail today because they are not in npm run check: (1) npm --prefix client run test:parity FAILS on 21 ambulance meshes (veh|medic|0..7 y-pos 1.6 vs 1.455 against 353a5b7616f2); (2) census.mjs self-test FAILS on 3 assertions (expects 36 headless checks vs 87, 57 unmapped routes, 147 unmapped files), has a temp-dir bug in lineCoverage(), and ignores mcp/ & audit/.
01 · Live Verification Scorecard & Test Pyramid

Every Suite Executed Live During This Audit

Each test runner was executed against the current working tree, real PostgreSQL (freightchain_test on :15433), real Redis (:16379), headless Chrome (SwiftShader WebGL), and compiled SBPF v0 Solana programs (freightchain_pools.so + freightchain_honk.so). Notably, .claude/skills/test-census/census.mjs only scans 550 files because its hardcoded regexes omit mcp/test/*.test.mjs (4 files, 12 tests) and audit/ (46 files, 245 tests).

03 · Headless 60 Hz Playthrough & 3D Scene-Tree Parity

How headless-test.mjs, stub-three.mjs & parity/run.mjs Work

Because Freight Chain's client is vanilla ES modules + Three.js r160 with no build step, client/tools/trace.sh uses esbuild to bundle client/js/main.js into a temporary single-file ESM bundle with --alias:three=./tools/stub-three.mjs. This allows Node.js to execute a complete 60 Hz driving playthrough over 60+ km of road in 1.8 seconds without a browser or GPU.

1. Simulated Browser & 60 Hz Clock (headless-test.mjs) Node ESM

  • Deterministic PRNG: Replaces Math.random with a seeded Linear Congruential Generator (seed = (seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0, starting at 12345) alongside mulberry32 in @freightchain/shared.
  • Simulated 60 Hz Frame Stepper: performance.now() starts at 1000; requestAnimationFrame captures the game's frame callback rafCb; step(n) advances now += 1000 / 60 (16.667 ms) and invokes rafCb(now) n times.
  • Scripted Driver (steerCtl, drive, crawl): Temporarily sets G.CFG.TRUCK to pre-weight handling so the bang-bang lane-keeping controller (e = (st.d - 2.4) + 10 * st.psi, updated every 6 frames) can exit the depot and cruise 60 km of highway; restores shipped TRUCK0 physics at line 70 before testing clutch/gear launches and stalls.

2. In-Memory Three.js & Canvas Stubs (stub-three.mjs) Zero WebGL

  • Scene Graph (Obj, Group, Scene, Mesh, V): Implements 3D vectors (x, y, z, project()), Euler rotations, parent/child hierarchies, traverse(), and visibility flags in plain JS objects. WebGLRenderer.render() simply increments this.calls.
  • Proxy 2D Canvas (ctxStub) & Fake DOM (El): El.getContext('2d') returns a Proxy that swallows all 2D cockpit drawing calls while returning valid gradient objects and measureText() -> { width: 10 }.
  • 3D Parity Recorder (tools/parity/): three-rec.mjs records every geometry mutation (rotateX/Y/Z, translate, scale) and material option, while scene-shim.mjs hashes every 2D CanvasTexture draw log with SHA-1 to compare 11,643 3D objects against baseline commit 353a5b7616f2.
Failing Gate · npm --prefix client run test:parity (21 of 11,643 Objects Differ)

Running node client/tools/parity/run.mjs against client/tools/parity/BASE (353a5b7616f2) currently exits with code 1: 11,643 objects compared, 21 differ. Specifically, the ambulance entity (veh|medic|0..7 and veh|medic|siren) has r.29 MeshP y-position 1.6 in current vs 1.455 in legacy (plus roof cross/lightbar offsets). Either the ambulance entity edit was intentional and tools/parity/BASE needs to be bumped, or the ambulance entity regression should be reverted.

04 · Blockchain & Solana On-Chain Program Verification

197 Rust Tests Across freightchain_pools (157) & freightchain_honk (40)

Both Anchor programs are compiled to SBPF v0 (anchor build --arch v0) and tested in-process using LiteSVM 0.10.0 (184 integration tests executing real SBF bytecode, CPIs, and PDA derivations) plus 13 pure Rust unit tests, and then verified end-to-end against a real solana-test-validator in 6 localnet E2E harnesses.

How LiteSVM Mocks the Solana Runtime (tests/common/mod.rs) 184 Tests

  • SBPF v0 Binary Guard: Embeds target/deploy/freightchain_pools.so & freightchain_honk.so, checks ELF header bytes[0x30..0x34] == [0,0,0,0], and loads via svm.add_program(PID, bytes).
  • BPFLoaderUpgradeable ProgramData Forging: Derives the ProgramData PDA and writes bincode tag [3,0,0,0] + upgrade authority pubkey at bytes 12..45 (or 0 at byte 12 in make_program_immutable) to test initialize authority checks.
  • Deterministic Clock & SlotHashes Sysvars: warp(ts) and set_slot(slot) mutate the Clock sysvar; set_slot_hashes() manually serializes bincode (u64 len, [(u64 slot, [u8;32] hash)]) into SysvarS1otHashes111... to test v4 seal_road window edges, skipped leader slots, and SealRearmed.
  • Direct Account Forging (svm.set_account): Truncates Config to 118 bytes and settled Pool accounts to v3 size to test live v3→v4 migration; forges u64::MAX/i64::MAX in pool counters to prove MathOverflow guards; uses historical_rules() & cover_player_share() for v5's <50% player-share ceiling.

LiteSVM vs. Real solana-test-validator Localnet E2Es 6 E2E Suites

  • What LiteSVM tests best: Exhaustive instruction/error matrices (e.g., 625-combination 5^4 adversarial Merkle matrix in settle.rs, 50-run pledge conservation fuzzing in pledge.rs, 14-day SETTLE_GRACE_SECS warps, CU ceilings, and forged overflow states).
  • What only solana-test-validator E2Es test: Real JSON-RPC & WebSocket logsSubscribe event streaming, blockhash expiration, Helius webhook race vs. poller, RPC 429/outage recovery via localnet-fault-proxy.mjs (e2e-localnet-chaos.mjs), real Chrome wallet signing, and multi-cranker races.
  • localnet-fixtures.mjs Pre-Baked Accounts: Because solana-test-validator cannot warp 14 days in wall time without expiring blockhashes, localnet-e2e/run.sh dumps two pools with solana account --output json-compact, shifts their deadlines back 14 days in raw account bytes, and reloads them via --account at validator boot.

05 · Deep Dive: Mocked, Stubbed & Faked Stuff Across the Codebase

What Is Faked vs. Real — And Where Mocks Have Blind Spots

Freight Chain deliberately avoids heavy mocking frameworks in business logic, preferring stateful in-memory twins (MemoryDb, FakePoolChain, stub-three.mjs) wired into unitContext() and createHarness('memory'). However, every test double simplifies reality in specific ways. The table below documents every mock, stub, and fake in the repository, how it works, and its exact divergence from production.

Mock / Fake / Stub File Location Used By How It Works & What It Simulates Blind Spots / Divergence from Production
Server In-Memory Persistence & Unit Context (server/test/support/)
MemoryDb & 34 Memory* Repositories server/test/support/in-memory/*.ts (14 files) unitContext() (204 specs) & createHarness('memory') (6 HTTP specs) Implements all 34 repository ports using JS Map/Array collections in MemoryDb, simulating optimistic concurrency version checks, unique constraints, and upserts in ~1ms per test. No rollback & no row locks: MemoryTransaction.run(fn) is a direct pass-through fn() — if a handler throws halfway through a transaction, prior writes to MemoryDb are not rolled back! Likewise, findByWalletForUpdate, findByIdForUpdate, and lockOpen are non-locking reads in memory. (Mitigated by running all 30 scenario suites + 54 race tests on real PostgreSQL in server:integration.)
FakeClock server/test/support/fake-clock.ts All server unit, HTTP & PG integration tests Implements ClockPort starting at 2036-10-08T12:00:00Z (10 years in the future) with deterministic advanceMs() / advanceSeconds() / set(). Starts in 2036 so Supertest's cookie jar (which compares cookie Expires against real wall-clock Date.now()) never drops session cookies when tests warp time forward.
FakeChain server/test/support/fake-chain.ts Transfer-mode ride & donation tests Builds real unsigned Solana transfer+memo+reference wire transactions while storing scripted ChainTransaction records and per-reference signature histories in memory (addPayment, addHistory). Does not verify Ed25519 transaction signatures on chain or simulate Solana blockhash expiry unless explicitly scripted.
FakePoolChain, FakePoolCrankChain & FakePoolPayoutChain server/test/support/fake-pool-*.ts Escrow pool, ticket, crank, payout & pledge specs Builds real unsigned program transactions (buy_ticket, refund, commit_winners, pay_prize, create_pledge) and simulates on-chain pool/pledge state transitions, RFC 6962 Merkle proof verification, review lock payFrom, onSigned crash window, and RPC outages (rpcDown = true). Does not execute Rust SBF instructions or check compute-unit / rent-exemption math (e.g., Audit H4 unfunded driver rent minimum or v5 ticket_excess). Those are verified in Rust LiteSVM + e2e-localnet*.mjs.
FakeMintChain server/test/support/fake-mint-chain.ts Achievement cNFT minting specs Simulates Metaplex Bubblegum Merkle tree capacity (treeUsage), leaf asset ID derivation, onSigned callback before confirmation, and transient RPC failures (failNext). No localnet validator test deploys the Metaplex Bubblegum program; real Bubblegum CPI wire encoding is only unit-tested in bubblegum-tx.spec.ts and bubblegum.mint-chain.adapter.spec.ts.
ScriptedLlm / FakeLlmAdapter & TestMcpClient server/test/support/editor-job-fixtures.ts, test-mcp.ts, fake-llm.adapter.ts Editor agent specs, editor-jobs.scenarios.ts, e2e-editor.mjs ScriptedLlm replays canned tool-call turns; EDITOR_FAKE_LLM=1 runs a deterministic 3-step MCP sequence (get_entity → append box → check_entity → save_draft); TestMcpClient routes MCP tool calls in-process over Supertest to /api/editor/mcp/*. Never hits live Anthropic/OpenAI/Gemini/xAI APIs during automated tests; production guard in validateEnv refuses EDITOR_FAKE_LLM=1 when NODE_ENV=production.
busMock() server/test/support/bus.ts All 32 presentation controller unit specs Replaces NestJS CommandBus and QueryBus with a spy recording calls[] when controllers are instantiated directly via new XController(cmd, qry). Bypassing Nest's HTTP pipeline in controller unit specs means @UseGuards, @ZodBody pipes, and exception filters do not run in test/unit/presentation/ (7 DTO schemas are not called there). Covered in test/http/ & test/scenarios/.
Disabled Background Schedulers in createHarness() server/test/support/harness.ts All test/http/ & test/integration/flows Overrides StaleRideScheduler, ReplayVerificationScheduler, HonkReleaseScheduler, and EditorJobSweepScheduler with empty {} objects. Prevents real setInterval timers from firing while tests manipulate FakeClock; scheduler classes are tested separately in test/unit/infrastructure/scheduler/*.spec.ts and live E2Es.
Client, Headless Playthrough & Browser E2E Mocks
stub-three.mjs, three-rec.mjs & scene-shim.mjs client/tools/stub-three.mjs, client/tools/parity/* headless-test.mjs, refactor-architecture.test.mjs, test:parity Replaces Three.js r160 CDN import with an in-memory scene graph (V, Obj, Mesh, Scene) and Proxy 2D canvas (ctxStub). Does not compile GLSL shaders, rasterize pixels, or test WebGL context loss (covered by real Chrome SwiftShader in e2e-chrome.mjs and e2e-viewer.mjs).
Injected Fake Phantom & Wallet Standard landing/tools/e2e-wallet.mjs, server/scripts/e2e-*.mjs All wallet-authenticated Chrome E2E suites Injects window.phantom.solana and Wallet Standard (wallet-standard:register-wallet) backed by real WebCrypto / tweetnacl Ed25519 signing inside the browser page. Real Ed25519 signatures and real SIWS wire payloads, but does not test real browser extension popup windows or Ledger hardware wallet frames.
cdp.mjs driveHook(speed) & answerPrompts(cdp) client/tools/cdp.mjs e2e-browser.mjs, e2e-replay.mjs, e2e-watch.mjs, etc. driveHook advances g.st.s & g.st.driveT inside requestAnimationFrame while holding real CDP keys; answerPrompts polls #prompt.on and dispatches real CDP Input.dispatchKeyEvent with 400–1200ms human jitter. driveHook bypasses full clutch/gearbox launch in browser E2Es that only care about telemetry batching and finish-line completion (full gearbox physics is tested in headless-test.mjs and e2e-chrome.mjs).
stub-api.mjs Stateful Fake Server server/scripts/stub-api.mjs e2e-tickets-stub.mjs (npm --prefix server run test:tickets-stub) 242-line standalone Node HTTP server mocking lobby/pool/ticket endpoints in memory without NestJS or Postgres. Currently out of sync with client UI: lacks POST /api/tickets/orders/:id/reconcile and GET /api/referrals, causing e2e-tickets-stub.mjs to fail 2 of 20 checks!
localnet-fixtures.mjs & Raw Account Byte-Patching server/scripts/localnet-fixtures.mjs, .claude/skills/localnet-e2e/run.sh e2e-localnet*.mjs Pre-builds raw Ed25519 wire transactions and patches dumped Pool account JSON bytes to shift deadlines back by 14 days (SETTLE_GRACE_SECS), loading them via solana-test-validator --account. Avoids waiting 14 real days on a live validator while still executing real on-chain expire_pool and grace-period close_pool instructions against the patched accounts.

07 · PostgreSQL Concurrency & Lock-Order Verification (54 Race Suites)

All 54 Multi-Writer Race Tests on Real PostgreSQL

Freight Chain's backend architecture enforces a strict lock order (player -> pool -> tickets / pledges via SELECT ... FOR UPDATE, plus optimistic version columns and PostgreSQL transaction advisory locks). Every critical section is stress-tested with Promise.all parallel bursts (PARALLEL = 8) against a real PostgreSQL database in concurrency.integration.spec.ts (49 races) and entity-concurrency.integration.spec.ts (5 races).

Complete Catalog of All 54 PostgreSQL Concurrency Race Tests (entity-concurrency E1–E5 + concurrency C1–C49)54 / 54 PASS
Race ID Integration Suite Concurrent Scenario, Lock Mechanism & Verified Invariant
E1 entity-concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel first edits (baseRevisionId: null) of a seed entity (cactus) — EntityRepositoryPort.lock (SELECT ... FOR UPDATE) serializes them; 1 wins (seq: 2, seed becomes seq: 1) and 7 fail with stale_base.
E2 entity-concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel drafts on the same existing head revision — 1 wins (seq: 3) and 7 fail with stale_base.
E3 entity-concurrency.integration.spec.ts 4 parallel McpSaveDraftCommand calls on the same running AI job — serialized under the job row lock (SELECT ... FOR UPDATE on EditorJob), advancing the job's tracked base revision each time so all 4 succeed sequentially (seq: 2, 3, 4, 5) without stale_base conflicts.
E4 entity-concurrency.integration.spec.ts Human saveDraft racing AI McpSaveDraftCommand from the same base — entity row lock serializes them; 1 wins (seq: 2) and the other fails with stale_base, preventing AI from silently overwriting a human edit.
E5 entity-concurrency.integration.spec.ts Two concurrent signed publishes (POST /api/editor/entities/:id/publish) where Editor A publishes birch referencing job-crate with slot m: 'trunk', and Editor B publishes a new revision of job-crate adding tag: 'trunk' (which together fail whole-catalogue cross-entity validation) — global publish advisory lock (pg_advisory_xact_lock) serializes catalog validation so one returns 200 and the second returns 422, keeping /api/entities/bundle valid.
C1 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel free-ride requests (POST /api/rides) → player row lock (findByWalletForUpdate) reuses the 1 created ride, 1 DB row, balance stays 10.
C2 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel PUT /api/profile/agreements with alternating {age18, loss} pairs → serialized on player lock; no torn state between age18/age18At and loss/lossAt.
C3 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel paid ride orders (tier: '1h') → 1 awaiting_payment order created and returned to all 8 callers.
C4 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 wallets placing the first paid order of a UTC day simultaneously → DailySeedPrismaRepository.claim (INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING) stores 1 DailySeed row and all 8 get the same road seed.
C5 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel paid ride orders while holding an unstarted free ride → cancels the free ride once, creates 1 awaiting_payment order, leaves freeRides at 10.
C6 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/rides/:id/start on a ticket ride at the play-window boundary (H6) → in time, 1 starts (200) and 7 get 409; past rideBy, 1 cancels the ride with 409 rides_closed and 7 see it already cancelled (409), no deadlock.
C7 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Ride promo code (POST /api/promo/redeem) racing GrantDailyFreeRidesCommand and GET /api/me → 1 redemption wins (200), 7 get 409, 1 PromoRedemption and 1 PromoRideGrant.
C8 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 wallets racing for the last redemption (maxRedemptions: 1) of a scheduled promo code 1s before and at startsAt → at startsAt - 1s all 8 get 409 (redeemed: 0); at startsAt 1 wins (200) and 7 get 409 (redeemed: 1).
C9 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel redemptions of a retired cosmetic promo code → all 8 get 409 visuals_are_honk, 0 unlocks.
C10 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 payout workers racing PoolPayoutRepositoryPort.claimSent for the same (poolId, rank) → conditional UPDATE ... WHERE status = 'planned' lets 1 win (attempts: 1).
C11 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Admin pools.saveHold racing 8 ledger.claimSentUnlessHeld calls (L11) → atomic SQL check against Pool.holdAt IS NULL ensures no claim succeeds after hold commits and held claims write nothing.
C12 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Admin pools.saveHold racing 8 pools.upsert chain syncs → ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE preserves holdBy/holdAt/holdNote columns so the hold survives.
C13 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel paid-ride orders spending 1 pool ticket → player lock + ticket version ensures 1 ride created, balance becomes 0, 1 spend grant row.
C14 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 wallets racing for a 1-use ticket promo code (LAST), and 1 wallet sending 8 parallel requests for an unlimited promo code (MINE, perWallet: 1) → 1 winner each.
C15 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Same TicketBought purchase event settled 8 times in parallel (TicketSettlementService.settlePurchase) → 1 applied, 7 duplicate, credited once (balance: 2, bought: 2).
C16 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 6 settlePurchase calls racing applyPoolEvent(PoolFailed) → consistent lock order (player → pool → tickets) prevents deadlock; pool ends refunding with balance: 0, bought: 6.
C17 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 4 ReceiveChainWebhookCommand + 4 PollProgramCommand settling the same tx (TicketBought + PoolActivated) concurrently → credited once, 1 order, pool activated once, 2 ChainEvent rows, cursor updated.
C18 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 4 FinalityService.drop calls racing POST /api/rides on unfinalized tickets → voided + ridden === 2 (never both voided and ridden), event marked dropped once.
C19 concurrency.integration.spec.ts tickets.voidAllOfPool racing 4 riders spending tickets via POST /api/rides → voided + ridden === 8, total remaining balance 0.
C20 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/pledges/:id/confirm racing 3 SyncPledgesCommand passes → all 8 confirms return 200 active, pledge activated once.
C21 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel SyncPledgesCommand passes racing to settle 1 pledge on chain → settle_pledge called once on FakePoolPayoutChain, pledge settled once with exact earned/refunded lamports.
C22 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel GET /api/me on a new UTC day → retired daily grant does not change freeRides.
C23 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 3 parallel GrantDailyFreeRidesCommand racing 8 GET /api/me and 8 POST /api/rides → freeRides unchanged.
C24 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel uploads of the same telemetry batch (seq: 0) → all 8 return 200, 1 has stored: true, 7 have stored: false, 1 DB row.
C25 concurrency.integration.spec.ts POST /api/rides/:id/end racing 4 parallel uploads of the final batch → ride lock serializes /end and batch re-judging so the final verdict sees all batches and is verified.
C26 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 2 VerdictService.addAttention calls racing sequential late batch uploads after /end → final verdict has seen all batches AND retains the pool-shared-ip flag.
C27 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Admin POST /api/admin/rides/:id/verdict override racing sequential late batch uploads → admin rejected override is preserved and not clobbered by late batch re-judging.
C28 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/auth/verify for one challenge → atomic DELETE ... RETURNING in ChallengePrismaRepository.consume lets 1 win (200) and 7 get 401.
C29 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel GET /api/pool at the turn of a new week → 1 new Round created, old round closed once.
C30 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/rides/:id/confirm of the same transfer payment → all 8 return 200 paid idempotently, 1 Payment row inserted.
C31 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 4 /confirm + 4 /reconcile racing on the same payment → all 8 return 200 paid, 1 Payment row.
C32 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Round carry invariant across week turn (3 /confirm calls racing 4 GET /api/pool calls at week boundary) → next round's carry equals exact 20% of closed round's entries, all 4 payments recorded.
C33 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Two wallets submitting the same transaction signature for two different rides in parallel → unique constraint on Payment.signature allows at most 1 payment row.
C34 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 spectators posting paid/mock stream effects (POST /api/watch/:id/donations) in the same rate-limit window → unique (rideId, slotKey, bucket) / advisory lock lets 1 succeed (200) and 7 get 429.
C35 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 1 spectator sending 8 parallel identical chat messages (POST /api/watch/:id/messages) → 3s bucket unique constraint lets 1 succeed (200) and 7 get 429.
C36 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/watch/:id/donation-orders/:orderId/confirm for a paid donation → all 8 return 200 with the same single RideEvent id.
C37 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/rides/:id/mutes (muted: true) by the driver → all 8 return 200, 1 RideMute row stored.
C38 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/rides/:id/effects/:eventId/applied acks by the driver → conditional update (WHERE appliedAt IS NULL) stamps once, all 8 return the winning stamp.
C39 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 2 SweepStaleRidesCommand passes racing a late /heartbeat and /end (4 rounds) → ride ends once, never stays running, no 500 error.
C40 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 parallel POST /api/rides/:id/start → 1 returns 200, 7 return 409.
C41 concurrency.integration.spec.ts Re-choosing free ride level (POST /api/rides with level: 3) racing POST /api/rides/:id/start (6 rounds) → player lock ensures atomic level (either started as level 1 and re-choose got 409, or re-chosen to level 3 and started as level 3; never a torn mix).
C42 concurrency.integration.spec.ts POST /api/rides/:id/end racing 4 parallel /heartbeat calls (5 rounds) → /end always succeeds (200) and ride is never left running.
C43 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 riders ending rides simultaneously → atomic INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE on stats.GlobalStats and stats.PlayerStats produces exact sums with zero drift (ReconcileStatsCommand).
C44 concurrency.integration.spec.ts SweepStaleRidesCommand, late /heartbeat, and /end racing on 1 ride → counted in PlayerStats and GlobalStats exactly once.
C45 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 8 spectators donating (effect: 'tip') in parallel → all 8 counted on driver's career.donations and global donations.
C46 concurrency.integration.spec.ts First donation to a driver racing the driver's first /end (when no PlayerStats row exists yet, 6 rounds) → player lock acquired before StatsService prevents upsert deadlock; ride and valid donation both counted.
C47 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 2 wallets racing POST /api/referrals/bind for the referrer's last invite slot (referral_max: 1) → referrer player lock serializes them; 1 binds (200) and 1 gets 409 no_slots.
C48 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 2 referred friends finishing MEDIUM rides concurrently (ReferralService.onVerified) under referral_free_ride_weekly_cap: 1 → both referrals become qualified, but only 1 gets freeGranted: true (+2 to referrer, +3 to that friend).
C49 concurrency.integration.spec.ts 2 spectators concurrently creating paid donation-orders for a referred driver when only 1 share's worth of referral_donation_cap_lamports remains → only 1 DonationOrder reserves the referrer share (shareState: 'reserved').

09 · Comprehensive Gaps, Failing Tests, Bugs & Recommendations

All Issues Discovered During This Audit (Prioritized P0 → P2)

P0 · Issue 1 — 3D Scene-Tree Parity Gate Fails (npm --prefix client run test:parity)

Symptom: node client/tools/parity/run.mjs exits with status 1: 11643 objects compared against 353a5b7616f2: 21 differ.

Root Cause: veh|medic|0..7 and veh|medic|siren (client/shared/entity/seed/vehicle/medic.js) have r.29 MeshP y-position 1.6 (vs 1.455 in baseline commit 353a5b7616f2) and updated roof lightbar/cross positions. Because npm --prefix client run check only runs lint + test:headless + test:unit (and does not run test:parity), this drift went unnoticed.

Fix: If the ambulance geometry change was intentional, update client/tools/parity/BASE to the current commit SHA; if unintentional, restore medic.js coordinates and add test:parity to client/package.json check.

P0 · Issue 2 — Lobby Tickets Stub Chrome E2E Fails 2 of 20 Checks (npm --prefix server run test:tickets-stub)

Symptom: node server/scripts/e2e-tickets-stub.mjs fails with 18 passed, 2 failed: FAIL: RETRY CONFIRM settles the pending order and FAIL: no page errors (got 4).

Root Cause: The lobby client (client/site/tickets-api.js & client/site/lobby.js) was updated to call POST /api/tickets/orders/:id/reconcile on RETRY CONFIRM and GET /api/referrals on lobby boot, but the standalone fake server server/scripts/stub-api.mjs only implements POST /api/tickets/orders/:id/confirm and has no route for /reconcile or /api/referrals, returning HTTP 404.

Fix: Add POST /api/tickets/orders/:id/reconcile (delegating to the confirm handler) and GET /api/referrals (returning an empty referral view) to server/scripts/stub-api.mjs.

P0 · Issue 3 — test-census Self-Test Fails, Has a Temp-Dir Bug in lineCoverage(), and Misses mcp/ & audit/

Symptom: node .claude/skills/test-census/census.mjs self-test fails on 3 assertions, and census.mjs undercounts the repo by 50 test files (257 test cases).

  • Stale assertion at census.mjs:1200: Asserts inv.layers.find(l => l.id === 'client-headless').checks === 36, whereas client/tools/headless-test.mjs now has 87 static check() calls (97 runtime).
  • 57 Unmapped Routes & 147 Unmapped Source Files (census.mjs:1286–1287): Features added in Oct 11–17 (3D Editor/Jobs/MCP, Entities, TEN4 honk, Solana Actions/Blinks, Pledges, Referrals, SNS, Solana Pay, Prize Board, Phone Pairing) have no entries in .claude/skills/test-census/features.mjs.
  • lineCoverage() Temp-Directory Ordering Bug (census.mjs:1101–1108): lineCoverage() creates client/.census-cov-<pid> inside client/ before running server unit + HTTP, and leaves out mcp/test and audit/scripts.
  • coverage.mjs Route Parser Blind Spots: requestsIn() (coverage.mjs:86) checks s.indexOf('/api/'), so it never attributes GET /actions.json; it also misses dynamic hrefs like agent.post(open.href) for POST /api/actions/ride/:id and helpers in test/support/test-mcp.ts for GET /api/editor/mcp/entities/:id.

  7. POST /api/watch/:id/honk/time (HonkHttpController#honkTime)

  • Server CLI & Worker Entrypoints: server/src/cli/admin-commands.ts (167 lines — runAdmin command dispatcher), server/src/cli/cli.ts (106 lines — migrate, db:reset, db:seed), and server/src/app/editor-worker.ts (26 lines) have 0% unit test coverage.
  • Achievement NFT Metadata Map: server/src/domain/achievements/nft-metadata-uris.ts has no unit test verifying that every achievement ID in ACHIEVEMENTS has a valid URI entry.
  • 7 Presentation DTO Files Skipped in Controller Unit Specs: live.dto.ts, lobby.dto.ts, client-error.dto.ts, telemetry.dto.ts, solana-pay.dto.ts, entities.dto.ts, and stats.dto.ts are not tested in their corresponding *.http.controller.spec.ts files.
  • Server HTTP Decorators & Middleware Without Unit Specs: client-info.ts, current-job.ts, current-player.ts, and solana-pay-cors.middleware.ts have no dedicated unit spec (only exercised via HTTP integration tests).
  • Client, Landing, MCP, Media & Audit Untested Files:
    • client/js/themes/_shared/profile.js is an orphan/dead file not imported anywhere in client/.

    • mcp/src/stdio.js (MCP stdio transport entrypoint) has no unit test.
    • 4 pitchdeck visual modules (landing/src/pitchdeck/{core,fx,road,ticker}.js) and 3 browser video-capture scripts (media/video/{capture.mjs,clock.js,page.js}) have no unit tests.
    • 9 helper/legacy files in audit/scripts/ (harness.mjs, report-browser.js, resolve-target.sh, run-pocs.mjs, sandbox.sh, session-smoke.mjs) have no unit test.
P2 · Issue 6 — Duplicate Scenario Registration, E2E Contention, On-Chain Gaps & Missing CI
  • analyticsScenarios Registered Twice: Both server/test/http/flows.spec.ts and server/test/integration/flows.integration.spec.ts register analyticsScenarios twice (once in the main suites table with PostHog off, and once in a separate describe block with PostHog on). Splitting the disabled vs. enabled assertions into two distinct scenario functions (analyticsOffScenarios and analyticsOnScenarios) would eliminate redundant test execution.
  • Dead Assertion in verdict.spec.ts: server/test/unit/domain/verdict.spec.ts:205 has a placeholder expect(RULES).toBeDefined() solely to silence an unused variable warning.
  • program-check Omits freightchain_honk: .claude/skills/program-check/check.sh only verifies IDL drift and reproducible build hashes for freightchain_pools, leaving freightchain_honk without a committed programs/idl/freightchain_honk.json drift check or verifiable build hash pin.
  • E2E SwiftShader CPU Contention & Tight Pool Deadlines: Suites spawning 3–4 concurrent headless Chrome instances under software WebGL (e2e-lobby.mjs, e2e-localnet.mjs, e2e-honk-chain.mjs) experience 0.3x–0.4x frame-rate slowdowns, which can race tight now + 8s..35s on-chain pool deadlines (6 failures recorded in the last full runs.json E2E run).
  • No Automated CI/CD Pipeline: There is no .github/workflows/ directory in the repo; all verification currently relies on manual execution before commit/deploy.
